5f:Tfb4,<p>A <dfn id="script-record" variants="Script Records">Script Record</dfn> encapsulates information about a script being evaluated. Each script record contains the fields listed in <emu-xref href="#table-script-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-script-records">Table 34</a></emu-xref>.</p>

      <emu-table id="table-script-records" caption="Script Record Fields" data-num="34">
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>
                Field Name
              </th>
              <th>
                Value Type
              </th>
              <th>
                Meaning
              </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tr>
            <td>
              <var class="field">[[Realm]]</var>
            </td>
            <td>
              a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref>
            </td>
            <td>
              The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref> within which this script was created.
            </td>
          </tr>
          <tr>
            <td>
              <var class="field">[[ECMAScriptCode]]</var>
            </td>
            <td>
              a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>
            </td>
            <td>
              The result of parsing the source text of this script.
            </td>
          </tr>
          <tr>
            <td>
              <var class="field">[[LoadedModules]]</var>
            </td>
            <td>
              a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record">LoadedModuleRequest Records</a></emu-xref>
            </td>
            <td>
              A map from the specifier strings imported by this script to the resolved <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>. The list does not contain two different <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Records</a></emu-xref> <var>r1</var> and <var>r2</var> such that <emu-xref aoid="ModuleRequestsEqual"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-ModuleRequestsEqual">ModuleRequestsEqual</a></emu-xref>(<var>r1</var>, <var>r2</var>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.
            </td>
          </tr>
          <tr>
            <td>
              <var class="field">[[HostDefined]]</var>
            </td>
            <td>
              anything (default value is <emu-const>empty</emu-const>)
            </td>
            <td>
              Field reserved for use by <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environments</a></emu-xref> that need to associate additional information with a script.
            </td>
          </tr>
        </table>
      </emu-table>

60:Tb30,<p>The abstract operation ParseScript takes arguments <var>sourceText</var> (a String or a sequence of Unicode code points), <var>realm</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref>), and <var>hostDefined</var> (anything) and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record">Script Record</a></emu-xref> or a non-empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-val>SyntaxError</emu-val> objects. It creates a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record">Script Record</a></emu-xref> based upon the result of parsing <var>sourceText</var> as a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt>. It performs the following steps when called:</p>

      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>script</var> be <emu-xref aoid="ParseText"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-parsetext">ParseText</a></emu-xref>(<var>sourceText</var>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt>).</li><li class="exit">If <var>script</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of errors, return <var>script</var>.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record">Script Record</a></emu-xref> { <var class="field">[[Realm]]</var>: <var>realm</var>, <var class="field">[[ECMAScriptCode]]</var>: <var>script</var>, <var class="field">[[LoadedModules]]</var>: « », <var class="field">[[HostDefined]]</var>: <var>hostDefined</var> }.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>An implementation may parse script source text and analyse it for Early Error conditions prior to evaluation of ParseScript for that script source text. However, the reporting of any errors must be deferred until the point where this specification actually performs ParseScript upon that source text.</p>

62:T5966,<p>The abstract operation GlobalDeclarationInstantiation takes arguments <var>script</var> (a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>) and <var>envRecord</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-global-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-global-environment-records">Global Environment Record</a></emu-xref>) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>. <var>script</var> is the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt> for which the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> is being established. <var>envRecord</var> is the global environment in which bindings are to be created.</p>
      <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>When an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> is established for evaluating scripts, declarations are instantiated in the current global environment. Each global binding declared in the code is instantiated.</p>

      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/error-handling-and-language-extensions#early-error"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/error-handling-and-language-extensions#early-error">Early errors</a></emu-xref> specified in <emu-xref href="#sec-scripts-static-semantics-early-errors"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-scripts-static-semantics-early-errors">16.1.1</a></emu-xref> prevent name conflicts between function/var declarations and let/const/class declarations as well as redeclaration of let/const/class bindings for declaration contained within a single <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt>. However, such conflicts and redeclarations that span more than one <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt> are detected as runtime errors during GlobalDeclarationInstantiation. If any such errors are detected, no bindings are instantiated for the script. However, if the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref> is defined using <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#proxy-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#proxy-exotic-object">Proxy exotic objects</a></emu-xref> then the runtime tests for conflicting declarations may be unreliable resulting in an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">abrupt completion</a></emu-xref> and some global declarations not being instantiated. If this occurs, the code for the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt> is not evaluated.</p>
        <p>Unlike explicit var or function declarations, properties that are directly created on the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref> result in global bindings that may be shadowed by let/const/class declarations.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

75:T782,<p>The abstract operation ImportedLocalNames takes argument <var>importEntries</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importentry-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importentry-record">ImportEntry Records</a></emu-xref>) and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of Strings. It creates a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of all of the local name bindings defined by <var>importEntries</var>. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>localNames</var> be a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>For each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importentry-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importentry-record">ImportEntry Record</a></emu-xref> <var>i</var> of <var>importEntries</var>, do<ol><li>Append <var>i</var>.<var class="field">[[LocalName]]</var> to <var>localNames</var>.</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <var>localNames</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

76:T14be,<p>A <dfn id="modulerequest-record" variants="ModuleRequest Records">ModuleRequest Record</dfn> represents the request to import a module with given import attributes. It consists of the following fields:</p>

              <td>
                a String
              </td>
              <td>
                The module specifier
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[Attributes]]</var>

              </td>
            </tr>

78:T428d,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> ModuleRequests takes no arguments and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Records</a></emu-xref>. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
        <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-Module" name="Module" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-constraints>[empty]</emu-constraints></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>

      67:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-static-semantics-modulerequests","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"16214-static-semantics-modulerequests","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.4"}]," Static Semantics: ModuleRequests"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"16214-static-semantics-modulerequests"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$78"}}]]}]
79:T34a2,<p>A <dfn variants="Module Records">Module Record</dfn> encapsulates structural information about the imports and exports of a single module. This information is used to link the imports and exports of sets of connected modules. A Module Record includes four fields that are only used when evaluating a module.</p>
        <p>For specification purposes Module Record values are values of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a></emu-xref> specification type and can be thought of as existing in a simple object-oriented hierarchy where Module Record is an abstract class with both abstract and concrete subclasses. This specification defines the abstract subclass named <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref> and its concrete subclass named <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record">Source Text Module Record</a></emu-xref>. Other specifications and implementations may define additional Module Record subclasses corresponding to alternative module definition facilities that they defined.</p>
        <p>Module Record defines the fields listed in <emu-xref href="#table-module-record-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-module-record-fields">Table 38</a></emu-xref>. All Module Definition subclasses include at least those fields. Module Record also defines the abstract method list in <emu-xref href="#table-abstract-methods-of-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-abstract-methods-of-module-records">Table 39</a></emu-xref>. All Module definition subclasses must provide concrete implementations of these abstract methods.</p>

              </td>
              <td>
                The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">Realm</a></emu-xref> within which this module was created.

            <tr id="abstract-resolveexport">
              <td>
                ResolveExport (
                  <var>exportName</var>: a String,
                  optional <var>resolveSet</var>: a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Records</a></emu-xref> with fields <var class="field">[[Module]]</var> (a Module Record) and <var class="field">[[ExportName]]</var> (a String),
                ): a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record">ResolvedBinding Record</a></emu-xref>, <emu-val>null</emu-val>, or <emu-const>ambiguous</emu-const>
              </td>
              <td>
                <p>It returns the binding of a name exported by this module. Bindings are represented by a <dfn id="resolvedbinding-record" variants="ResolvedBinding Records">ResolvedBinding Record</dfn>, of the form { <var class="field">[[Module]]</var>: Module Record, <var class="field">[[BindingName]]</var>: String | <emu-const>namespace</emu-const> }. If the export is a Module Namespace Object without a direct binding in any module, <var class="field">[[BindingName]]</var> will be set to <emu-const>namespace</emu-const>. It returns <emu-val>null</emu-val> if the name cannot be resolved, or <emu-const>ambiguous</emu-const> if multiple bindings were found.</p>
                <p>Each time this operation is called with a specific <var>exportName</var>, <var>resolveSet</var> pair as arguments it must return the same result.</p>
                <p>LoadRequestedModules must have completed successfully prior to invoking this method.</p>
              </td>
              <td>
                Within this specification it has definitions in the following types; <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> may provide additional types with their own definitions:
                <emu-concrete-method-dfns for="ResolveExport"></emu-concrete-method-dfns>
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr id="abstract-link">
              <td>Link ( ): either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref></td>
              <td>
                <p>It prepares the module for evaluation by transitively resolving all module dependencies and creating a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-module-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-module-environment-records">Module Environment Record</a></emu-xref>.</p>
                <p>LoadRequestedModules must have completed successfully prior to invoking this method.</p>
              </td>
              <td>
                Within this specification it has definitions in the following types; <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> may provide additional types with their own definitions:
                <emu-concrete-method-dfns for="Link"></emu-concrete-method-dfns>
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr id="abstract-evaluate">
              <td>Evaluate ( ): a Promise</td>
              <td>
                <p>It returns a promise for the evaluation of this module and its dependencies, resolving on successful evaluation or if it has already been evaluated successfully, and rejecting for an evaluation error or if it has already been evaluated unsuccessfully. If the promise is rejected, <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> are expected to handle the promise rejection and rethrow the evaluation error. Unless this module is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref>, the returned promise must be already settled.</p>
                <p>Link must have completed successfully prior to invoking this method.</p>
              </td>
              <td>
                Within this specification it has definitions in the following types; <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> may provide additional types with their own definitions:
                <emu-concrete-method-dfns for="Evaluate"></emu-concrete-method-dfns>
              </td>
            </tr>

        68:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-abstract-module-records","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"16215-abstract-module-records","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.5"}]," Abstract Module Records"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"16215-abstract-module-records"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$79"}}],"$L7a"]}]
7b:T5e3f,<p>A <dfn id="cyclic-module-record" variants="Cyclic Module Records">Cyclic Module Record</dfn> is used to represent information about a module that can participate in dependency cycles with other modules that are subclasses of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref> type. <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Records</a></emu-xref> that are not subclasses of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref> type must not participate in dependency cycles with <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record">Source Text Module Records</a></emu-xref>.</p>
        <p>In addition to the fields defined in <emu-xref href="#table-module-record-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-module-record-fields">Table 38</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Records</a></emu-xref> have the additional fields listed in <emu-xref href="#table-cyclic-module-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-cyclic-module-fields">Table 40</a></emu-xref></p>
        <emu-table id="table-cyclic-module-fields" caption="Additional Fields of Cyclic Module Records" data-num="40">

              <td>
                <var class="field">[[Status]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                <emu-const>new</emu-const>, <emu-const>unlinked</emu-const>, <emu-const>linking</emu-const>, <emu-const>linked</emu-const>, <emu-const>evaluating</emu-const>, <emu-const>evaluating-async</emu-const>, or <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const>
              </td>
              <td>
                Initially <emu-const>new</emu-const>. Transitions to <emu-const>unlinked</emu-const>, <emu-const>linking</emu-const>, <emu-const>linked</emu-const>, <emu-const>evaluating</emu-const>, possibly <emu-const>evaluating-async</emu-const>, <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const> (in that order) as the module progresses throughout its lifecycle. <emu-const>evaluating-async</emu-const> indicates this module is queued to execute on completion of its asynchronous dependencies or it is a module whose <var class="field">[[HasTLA]]</var> field is <emu-val>true</emu-val> that has been executed and is pending top-level completion.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[EvaluationError]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref> or <emu-const>empty</emu-const>
              </td>
              <td>
                A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref> representing the exception that occurred during evaluation. <emu-const>empty</emu-const> if no exception occurred or if <var class="field">[[Status]]</var> is not <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const>.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[DFSAncestorIndex]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer">integer</a></emu-xref> or <emu-const>empty</emu-const>
              </td>
              <td>
                Auxiliary field used during Link and Evaluate only. If <var class="field">[[Status]]</var> is either <emu-const>linking</emu-const> or <emu-const>evaluating</emu-const>, this is either the module's depth-first traversal index or that of an “earlier” module in the same strongly connected component.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[RequestedModules]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Records</a></emu-xref>
              </td>
              <td>
                A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Records</a></emu-xref> associated with the imports in this module. The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> is in source text occurrence order of the imports.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[LoadedModules]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record">LoadedModuleRequest Records</a></emu-xref>
              </td>
              <td>
                A map from the specifier strings used by the module represented by this record to request the importation of a module with the relative import attributes to the resolved <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>. The list does not contain two different <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Records</a></emu-xref> <var>r1</var> and <var>r2</var> such that <emu-xref aoid="ModuleRequestsEqual"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-ModuleRequestsEqual">ModuleRequestsEqual</a></emu-xref>(<var>r1</var>, <var>r2</var>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[CycleRoot]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-const>empty</emu-const>
              </td>
              <td>
                The first visited module of the cycle, the root DFS ancestor of the strongly connected component. For a module not in a cycle, this would be the module itself. Once Evaluate has completed, a module's <var class="field">[[DFSAncestorIndex]]</var> is the depth-first traversal index of its <var class="field">[[CycleRoot]]</var>.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[HasTLA]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a Boolean
              </td>
              <td>
                Whether this module is individually asynchronous (for example, if it's a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record">Source Text Module Record</a></emu-xref> containing a top-level await). Having an asynchronous dependency does not mean this field is <emu-val>true</emu-val>. This field must not change after the module is parsed.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                <emu-const>unset</emu-const>, an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer">integer</a></emu-xref>, or <emu-const>done</emu-const>
              </td>
              <td>
                This field is initially set to <emu-const>unset</emu-const>, and remains <emu-const>unset</emu-const> for fully synchronous modules. For modules that are either themselves asynchronous or have an asynchronous dependency, it is set to an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer">integer</a></emu-xref> that determines the order in which execution of pending modules is queued by <emu-xref href="#sec-async-module-execution-fulfilled"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-async-module-execution-fulfilled">16.2.1.6.1.3.4</a></emu-xref>. Once the pending module is successfully executed, the field is set to <emu-const>done</emu-const>.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[TopLevelCapability]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records">PromiseCapability Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-const>empty</emu-const>
              </td>
              <td>
                If this module is the <var class="field">[[CycleRoot]]</var> of some cycle, and Evaluate() was called on some module in that cycle, this field contains the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records">PromiseCapability Record</a></emu-xref> for that entire evaluation. It is used to settle the Promise object that is returned from the Evaluate() abstract method. This field will be <emu-const>empty</emu-const> for any dependencies of that module, unless a top-level Evaluate() has been initiated for some of those dependencies.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[AsyncParentModules]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Records</a></emu-xref>
              </td>
              <td>
                If this module or a dependency has <var class="field">[[HasTLA]]</var> <emu-val>true</emu-val>, and execution is in progress, this tracks the parent importers of this module for the top-level execution job. These parent modules will not start executing before this module has successfully completed execution.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[PendingAsyncDependencies]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer">integer</a></emu-xref> or <emu-const>empty</emu-const>
              </td>
              <td>
                If this module has any asynchronous dependencies, this tracks the number of asynchronous dependency modules remaining to execute for this module. A module with asynchronous dependencies will be executed when this field reaches 0 and there are no execution errors.
              </td>
            </tr>
          </table>
        </emu-table>
        <p>In addition to the methods defined in <emu-xref href="#table-abstract-methods-of-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-abstract-methods-of-module-records">Table 39</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Records</a></emu-xref> have the additional methods listed in <emu-xref href="#table-cyclic-module-methods"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-cyclic-module-methods">Table 41</a></emu-xref>:</p>

              <td>InitializeEnvironment ( ): either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref></td>
              <td>
                It initializes the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> of the module, including resolving all imported bindings, and creates the module's <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref>.
              </td>
              <td>

                <emu-concrete-method-dfns for="InitializeEnvironment"></emu-concrete-method-dfns>

            </tr>
            <tr id="abstract-executemodule">
              <td>

                  optional <var>capability</var>: a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records">PromiseCapability Record</a></emu-xref>,
                ): either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>
              </td>
              <td>
                It evaluates the module's code within its <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref>. If this module has <emu-val>true</emu-val> in <var class="field">[[HasTLA]]</var>, then a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records">PromiseCapability Record</a></emu-xref> is passed as an argument, and the method is expected to resolve or reject the given capability. In this case, the method must not throw an exception, but instead reject the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records">PromiseCapability Record</a></emu-xref> if necessary.
              </td>
              <td>
                Within this specification it has definitions in the following types; <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> may provide additional types with their own definitions:
                <emu-concrete-method-dfns for="ExecuteModule"></emu-concrete-method-dfns>
              </td>
            </tr>

        <p>A <dfn id="graphloadingstate-record" variants="GraphLoadingState Records">GraphLoadingState Record</dfn> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a></emu-xref> that contains information about the loading process of a module graph. It's used to continue loading after a call to <emu-xref aoid="HostLoadImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-HostLoadImportedModule">HostLoadImportedModule</a></emu-xref>. Each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#graphloadingstate-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#graphloadingstate-record">GraphLoadingState Record</a></emu-xref> has the fields defined in <emu-xref href="#table-graphloadingstate-record-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-graphloadingstate-record-fields">Table 42</a></emu-xref>:</p>
        <emu-table id="table-graphloadingstate-record-fields" caption="GraphLoadingState Record Fields" data-num="42">
          <table>

                </th>

                </th>

            <tr>

              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records">PromiseCapability Record</a></emu-xref>
              </td>
              <td>
                The promise to resolve when the loading process finishes.
              </td>

            <tr>
              <td>

              </td>

                a Boolean
              </td>
              <td>
                It is true if the loading process has not finished yet, neither successfully nor with an error.
              </td>

              <td>
                <var class="field">[[PendingModulesCount]]</var>
              </td>

                It tracks the number of pending <emu-xref aoid="HostLoadImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-HostLoadImportedModule">HostLoadImportedModule</a></emu-xref> calls.

              </td>

              </td>
              <td>
                It is a list of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Records</a></emu-xref> that have been already loaded by the current loading process, to avoid infinite loops with circular dependencies.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[HostDefined]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                anything (default value is <emu-const>empty</emu-const>)
              </td>
              <td>

              </td>
            </tr>

        69:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-cyclic-module-records","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"16216-cyclic-module-records","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.6"}]," Cyclic Module Records"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"16216-cyclic-module-records"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$7b"}}],"$L7c","$L7d"]}]
7e:T6a63,<p>A <dfn id="sourctextmodule-record" variants="Source Text Module Records">Source Text Module Record</dfn> is used to represent information about a module that was defined from <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-source-text"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-source-text">ECMAScript source text</a></emu-xref> (<emu-xref href="#sec-ecmascript-language-source-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-ecmascript-language-source-code">11</a></emu-xref>) that was parsed using the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars">goal symbol</a></emu-xref> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt>. Its fields contain digested information about the names that are imported and exported by the module, and its concrete methods use these digests to link and evaluate the module.</p>

        <p>A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record">Source Text Module Record</a></emu-xref> can exist in a module graph with other subclasses of the abstract <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref> type, and can participate in cycles with other subclasses of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref> type.</p>

        <p>In addition to the fields defined in <emu-xref href="#table-cyclic-module-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-cyclic-module-fields">Table 40</a></emu-xref>, <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record">Source Text Module Records</a></emu-xref> have the additional fields listed in <emu-xref href="#table-additional-fields-of-source-text-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-additional-fields-of-source-text-module-records">Table 52</a></emu-xref>. Each of these fields is initially set in <emu-xref aoid="ParseModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-parsemodule">ParseModule</a></emu-xref>.</p>

          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>

                </th>

                </th>

                  Meaning
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

              <td>
                <var class="field">[[ECMAScriptCode]]</var>
              </td>

              </td>
              <td>
                The result of parsing the source text of this module using <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt> as the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars">goal symbol</a></emu-xref>.
              </td>

              </td>

                <var class="field">[[ImportEntries]]</var>
              </td>

              </td>

              </td>

            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[LocalExportEntries]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record">ExportEntry Records</a></emu-xref>
              </td>

              </td>

            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[IndirectExportEntries]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record">ExportEntry Records</a></emu-xref>
              </td>
              <td>
                A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of ExportEntry records derived from the code of this module that correspond to reexported imports that occur within the module or exports from <code>export * as namespace</code> declarations.
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <var class="field">[[StarExportEntries]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record">ExportEntry Records</a></emu-xref>
              </td>
              <td>
                A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of ExportEntry records derived from the code of this module that correspond to <code>export *</code> declarations that occur within the module, not including <code>export * as namespace</code> declarations.
              </td>
            </tr>
          </table>
        </emu-table>
        <p>An <dfn id="importentry-record" variants="ImportEntry Records">ImportEntry Record</dfn> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a></emu-xref> that digests information about a single declarative import. Each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importentry-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importentry-record">ImportEntry Record</a></emu-xref> has the fields defined in <emu-xref href="#table-importentry-record-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-importentry-record-fields">Table 53</a></emu-xref>:</p>

              </tr>

                <var class="field">[[ModuleRequest]]</var>

              </td>
              <td>
                <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref> representing the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-ModuleSpecifier">ModuleSpecifier</a></emu-nt> and import attributes of the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-ImportDeclaration">ImportDeclaration</a></emu-nt>.

              </td>

        <p>An <dfn id="exportentry-record" variants="ExportEntry Records">ExportEntry Record</dfn> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a></emu-xref> that digests information about a single declarative export. Each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record">ExportEntry Record</a></emu-xref> has the fields defined in <emu-xref href="#table-exportentry-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-exportentry-records">Table 55</a></emu-xref>:</p>

                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>null</emu-val>

        <p>The following definitions specify the required concrete methods and other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-abstract-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-abstract-operations">abstract operations</a></emu-xref> for <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record">Source Text Module Records</a></emu-xref></p>

        6a:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-source-text-module-records","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"16217-source-text-module-records","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.7"}]," Source Text Module Records"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"16217-source-text-module-records"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$7e"}}],"$L7f","$L80","$L81"]}]
82:Tb19,<p>A <dfn variants="Synthetic Module Records">Synthetic Module Record</dfn> is used to represent information about a module that is defined by specifications. Its exported names are statically defined at creation, while their corresponding values can change over time using <emu-xref aoid="SetSyntheticModuleExport"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-setsyntheticmoduleexport">SetSyntheticModuleExport</a></emu-xref>. It has no imports or dependencies.</p>

        <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">A Synthetic Module Record could be used for defining a variety of module types: for example, JSON modules or CSS modules.</div></emu-note>

        <p>In addition to the fields defined in <emu-xref href="#table-module-record-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-module-record-fields">Table 38</a></emu-xref> Synthetic Module Records have the additional fields listed in <emu-xref href="#table-synthetic-module-record-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-synthetic-module-record-fields">Table 57</a></emu-xref>.</p>

        <emu-table id="table-synthetic-module-record-fields" caption="Additional Fields of Synthetic Module Records" data-num="57">
          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Field Name</th>
                <th>Value Type</th>
                <th>Meaning</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tr>
              <td><var class="field">[[ExportNames]]</var></td>
              <td>a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of Strings</td>
              <td>The names of the exports of the module. This list does not contain duplicates.</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td><var class="field">[[EvaluationSteps]]</var></td>
              <td>an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure">Abstract Closure</a></emu-xref></td>
              <td>The initialization logic to perform upon evaluation of the module, taking the Synthetic Module Record as its sole argument. It must not modify <var class="field">[[ExportNames]]</var>. It may return an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">abrupt completion</a></emu-xref>.</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
        </emu-table>

88:T8b2,<p>The abstract operation GetImportedModule takes arguments <var>referrer</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref>) and <var>request</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref>) and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>.  It performs the following steps when called:</p>

        <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>records</var> be a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> consisting of each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record">LoadedModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref> <var>r</var> of <var>referrer</var>.<var class="field">[[LoadedModules]]</var> such that <emu-xref aoid="ModuleRequestsEqual"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-ModuleRequestsEqual">ModuleRequestsEqual</a></emu-xref>(<var>r</var>, <var>request</var>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>records</var> has exactly one element, since LoadRequestedModules has completed successfully on <var>referrer</var> prior to invoking this abstract operation.</li><li>Let <var>record</var> be the sole element of <var>records</var>.</li><li class="exit">Return <var>record</var>.<var class="field">[[Module]]</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      6c:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-GetImportedModule","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"16219-getimportedmodule--referrer-request-","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.9"}]," GetImportedModule ( ",["$","var",null,{"children":"referrer"}],", ",["$","var",null,{"children":"request"}]," )"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"16219-getimportedmodule--referrer-request-"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$88"}}]]}]
89:T2995,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> abstract operation HostLoadImportedModule takes arguments <var>referrer</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record">Script Record</a></emu-xref>, a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref>, or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref>), <var>moduleRequest</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref>), <var>hostDefined</var> (anything), and <var>payload</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#graphloadingstate-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#graphloadingstate-record">GraphLoadingState Record</a></emu-xref> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-promisecapability-records">PromiseCapability Record</a></emu-xref>) and returns <emu-const>unused</emu-const>. </p>

        <emu-note id="note-HostLoadImportedModule-referrer-Realm-Record" data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>An example of when <var>referrer</var> can be a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref> is in a web browser <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref>. There, if a user clicks on a control given by</p>

          <pre><code class="hljs html"><span class="hljs-tag">&lt;<span class="hljs-name">button</span> <span class="hljs-attr">type</span>=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;button&quot;</span> <span class="hljs-attr">onclick</span>=<span class="hljs-string">&quot;import(&#x27;./foo.mjs&#x27;)&quot;</span>&gt;</span>Click me<span class="hljs-tag">&lt;/<span class="hljs-name">button</span>&gt;</span></code></pre>

          <p>there will be no <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job-activescriptormodule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job-activescriptormodule">active script or module</a></emu-xref> at the time the <emu-xref href="#sec-import-calls"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-import-calls"><code>import()</code></a></emu-xref> expression runs. More generally, this can happen in any situation where the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> pushes <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution contexts</a></emu-xref> with <emu-val>null</emu-val> ScriptOrModule components onto the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref>.</p>
        </div></emu-note>

        <p>An implementation of HostLoadImportedModule must conform to the following requirements:</p>
        <ul>
          <li>
            The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environment</a></emu-xref> must perform <emu-xref aoid="FinishLoadingImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-FinishLoadingImportedModule">FinishLoadingImportedModule</a></emu-xref>(<var>referrer</var>, <var>moduleRequest</var>, <var>payload</var>, <var>result</var>), where <var>result</var> is either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> the loaded <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>, either synchronously or asynchronously.
          </li>
          <li>
            <p>If this operation is called multiple times with two (<var>referrer</var>, <var>moduleRequest</var>) pairs such that:</p>
            <ul>
              <li>the first <var>referrer</var> is the same as the second <var>referrer</var>;</li>
              <li><emu-xref aoid="ModuleRequestsEqual"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-ModuleRequestsEqual">ModuleRequestsEqual</a></emu-xref>(the first <var>moduleRequest</var>, the second <var>moduleRequest</var>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>;</li>
            </ul>
            <p>and it performs <emu-xref aoid="FinishLoadingImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-FinishLoadingImportedModule">FinishLoadingImportedModule</a></emu-xref>(<var>referrer</var>, <var>moduleRequest</var>, <var>payload</var>, <var>result</var>) where <var>result</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion</a></emu-xref>, then it must perform <emu-xref aoid="FinishLoadingImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-FinishLoadingImportedModule">FinishLoadingImportedModule</a></emu-xref>(<var>referrer</var>, <var>moduleRequest</var>, <var>payload</var>, <var>result</var>) with the same <var>result</var> each time.</p>
          </li>
          <li>
            <p>If <var>moduleRequest</var>.<var class="field">[[Attributes]]</var> has an entry <var>entry</var> such that <var>entry</var>.<var class="field">[[Key]]</var> is <emu-val>"type"</emu-val> and <var>entry</var>.<var class="field">[[Value]]</var> is <emu-val>"json"</emu-val>, when the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environment</a></emu-xref> performs <emu-xref aoid="FinishLoadingImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-FinishLoadingImportedModule">FinishLoadingImportedModule</a></emu-xref>(<var>referrer</var>, <var>moduleRequest</var>, <var>payload</var>, <var>result</var>), <var>result</var> must either be the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">Completion Record</a></emu-xref> returned by an invocation of <emu-xref aoid="ParseJSONModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-parse-json-module">ParseJSONModule</a></emu-xref> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>.</p>
          </li>
          <li>
            The operation must treat <var>payload</var> as an opaque value to be passed through to <emu-xref aoid="FinishLoadingImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-FinishLoadingImportedModule">FinishLoadingImportedModule</a></emu-xref>.
          </li>
        </ul>

        <p>The actual process performed is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref>, but typically consists of performing whatever I/O operations are necessary to load the appropriate <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>. Multiple different (<var>referrer</var>, <var>moduleRequest</var>.<var class="field">[[Specifier]]</var>, <var>moduleRequest</var>.<var class="field">[[Attributes]]</var>) triples may map to the same <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref> instance. The actual mapping semantics is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> but typically a normalization process is applied to <var>specifier</var> as part of the mapping process. A typical normalization process would include actions such as expansion of relative and abbreviated path specifiers.</p>

8b:T587,<p>The abstract operation AllImportAttributesSupported takes argument <var>attrs</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importattribute-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importattribute-record">ImportAttribute Records</a></emu-xref>) and returns a Boolean.  It performs the following steps when called:</p>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>supported</var> be <emu-xref aoid="HostGetSupportedImportAttributes"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-hostgetsupportedimportattributes">HostGetSupportedImportAttributes</a></emu-xref>().</li><li>For each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importattribute-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#importattribute-record">ImportAttribute Record</a></emu-xref> <var>attr</var> of <var>attrs</var>, do<ol><li class="exit">If <var>supported</var> does not contain <var>attr</var>.<var class="field">[[Key]]</var>, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

        8c:Tab3,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> abstract operation HostGetSupportedImportAttributes takes no arguments and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of Strings. It allows <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environments</a></emu-xref> to specify which import attributes they support. Only attributes with supported keys will be provided to the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref>.</p>

          <p>An implementation of HostGetSupportedImportAttributes must conform to the following requirements:</p>

          <ul>
            <li>It must return a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of Strings, each indicating a supported attribute.</li>

            <li>Each time this operation is called, it must return the same <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> with the same contents in the same order.</li>
          </ul>

          <p>The default implementation of HostGetSupportedImportAttributes is to return a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.</p>

          <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">The purpose of requiring the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> to specify its supported import attributes, rather than passing all attributes to the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> and letting it then choose which ones it wants to handle, is to ensure that unsupported attributes are handled in a consistent way across different <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref>.</div></emu-note>
        6f:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-AllImportAttributesSupported","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"162112-allimportattributessupported--attrs-","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.12"}]," AllImportAttributesSupported ( ",["$","var",null,{"children":"attrs"}]," )"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"162112-allimportattributessupported--attrs-"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$8b"}}],["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-hostgetsupportedimportattributes","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"1621121-hostgetsupportedimportattributes--","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.12.1"}]," HostGetSupportedImportAttributes ( )"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"1621121-hostgetsupportedimportattributes--"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$8c"}}]]}]]}]
8d:Tb8d,<p>The abstract operation GetModuleNamespace takes argument <var>module</var> (an instance of a concrete subclass of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>) and returns a Module Namespace Object. It retrieves the Module Namespace Object representing <var>module</var>'s exports, lazily creating it the first time it was requested, and storing it in <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[Namespace]]</var> for future retrieval. It performs the following steps when called:</p>

        <emu-alg><ol><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: If <var>module</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref>, then <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> is not <emu-const>new</emu-const> or <emu-const>unlinked</emu-const>.</li><li>Let <var>namespace</var> be <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[Namespace]]</var>.</li><li>If <var>namespace</var> is <emu-const>empty</emu-const>, then<ol><li>Let <var>exportedNames</var> be <var>module</var>.GetExportedNames().</li><li>Let <var>unambiguousNames</var> be a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>For each element <var>name</var> of <var>exportedNames</var>, do<ol><li>Let <var>resolution</var> be <var>module</var>.ResolveExport(<var>name</var>).</li><li>If <var>resolution</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record">ResolvedBinding Record</a></emu-xref>, append <var>name</var> to <var>unambiguousNames</var>.</li></ol></li><li>Set <var>namespace</var> to <emu-xref aoid="ModuleNamespaceCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-modulenamespacecreate">ModuleNamespaceCreate</a></emu-xref>(<var>module</var>, <var>unambiguousNames</var>).</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <var>namespace</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
        <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>GetModuleNamespace never throws. Instead, unresolvable names are simply excluded from the namespace at this point. They will lead to a real linking error later unless they are all ambiguous star exports that are not explicitly requested anywhere.</p>
        </div></emu-note>
