9c:Ta90,<p>The abstract operation ModuleRequestsEqual takes arguments <var>x</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record">LoadedModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref>) and <var>y</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record">LoadedModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref>) and returns a Boolean. 9d:Ta41,<p>The abstract operation EvaluateModuleSync takes argument <var>module</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>. It synchronously evaluates <var>module</var>, provided that the caller guarantees that <var>module</var>'s evaluation will return an already settled promise. It performs the following steps when called:</p>

          <emu-alg><ol><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>module</var> is not a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>Let <var>promise</var> be <var>module</var>.Evaluate().</li><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>promise</var>.<var class="field">[[PromiseState]]</var> is either <emu-const>fulfilled</emu-const> or <emu-const>rejected</emu-const>.</li><li>If <var>promise</var>.<var class="field">[[PromiseState]]</var> is <emu-const>rejected</emu-const>, then<ol><li>If <var>promise</var>.<var class="field">[[PromiseIsHandled]]</var> is <emu-val>false</emu-val>, perform <emu-xref aoid="HostPromiseRejectionTracker"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-host-promise-rejection-tracker">HostPromiseRejectionTracker</a></emu-xref>(<var>promise</var>, <emu-val>"handle"</emu-val>).</li><li>Set <var>promise</var>.<var class="field">[[PromiseIsHandled]]</var> to <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">Throw <var>promise</var>.<var class="field">[[PromiseResult]]</var>.</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <emu-const>unused</emu-const>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
        7a:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-EvaluateModuleSync","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"162151-evaluatemodulesync--module-","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.5.1"}]," EvaluateModuleSync ( ",["$","var",null,{"children":"module"}]," )"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"162151-evaluatemodulesync--module-"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$9d"}}]]}]
9e:Tbcf,<p>The LoadRequestedModules concrete method of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref> <var>module</var> takes optional argument <var>hostDefined</var> (anything) and returns a Promise. It populates the <var class="field">[[LoadedModules]]</var> of all the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Records</a></emu-xref> in the dependency graph of <var>module</var> (most of the work is done by the auxiliary function <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLoading"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLoading">InnerModuleLoading</a></emu-xref>). It takes an optional <var>hostDefined</var> parameter that is passed to the <emu-xref aoid="HostLoadImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-HostLoadImportedModule">HostLoadImportedModule</a></emu-xref> hook. It performs the following steps when called:</p>

            <emu-alg><ol><li>If <var>hostDefined</var> is not present, set <var>hostDefined</var> to <emu-const>empty</emu-const>.</li><li>Let <var>promiseCapability</var> be ! <emu-xref aoid="NewPromiseCapability"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-newpromisecapability">NewPromiseCapability</a></emu-xref>(<emu-intrinsic>%Promise%</emu-intrinsic>).</li><li>Let <var>state</var> be the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#graphloadingstate-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#graphloadingstate-record">GraphLoadingState Record</a></emu-xref> { <var class="field">[[IsLoading]]</var>: <emu-val>true</emu-val>, <var class="field">[[PendingModulesCount]]</var>: 1, <var class="field">[[Visited]]</var>: « », <var class="field">[[PromiseCapability]]</var>: <var>promiseCapability</var>, <var class="field">[[HostDefined]]</var>: <var>hostDefined</var> }.</li><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLoading"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLoading">InnerModuleLoading</a></emu-xref>(<var>state</var>, <var>module</var>).</li><li class="exit">Return <var>promiseCapability</var>.<var class="field">[[Promise]]</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

            <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
              The <var>hostDefined</var> parameter can be used to pass additional information necessary to fetch the imported modules. It is used, for example, by HTML to set the correct fetch destination for <code>&lt;link rel="preload" as="..."&gt;</code> tags.
              <code>import()</code> expressions never set the <var>hostDefined</var> parameter.
            </div></emu-note>

            7c:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-cyclic-module-record-module-record-methods","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"162161-implementation-of-module-record-abstract-methods","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.6.1"}]," Implementation of Module Record Abstract Methods"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"162161-implementation-of-module-record-abstract-methods"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"<p>The following are the concrete methods for <emu-xref href=\"/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record\"><a href=\"/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record\">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref> that implement the corresponding <emu-xref href=\"/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records\"><a href=\"/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records\">Module Record</a></emu-xref> abstract methods defined in <emu-xref href=\"#table-abstract-methods-of-module-records\"><a href=\"/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-abstract-methods-of-module-records\">Table 39</a></emu-xref>.</p>\n\n          "}}],["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-LoadRequestedModules","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"1621611-loadrequestedmodules---hostdefined--","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"16.2.1.6.1.1"}]," LoadRequestedModules ( [ ",["$","var",null,{"children":"hostDefined"}]," ] )"],["$","$L55",null,{"id":"1621611-loadrequestedmodules---hostdefined--"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$9e"}}],"$L9f","$La0"]}],"$La1","$La2"]}]
a3:Ta216,<p>This non-normative section gives a series of examples of the linking and evaluation of a few common module graphs, with a specific focus on how errors can occur.</p>

          <p>First consider the following simple module graph:</p>

          <emu-figure id="figure-module-graph-simple" caption="A simple module graph" data-num="2">
            <img alt="A module graph in which module A depends on module B, and module B depends on module C" width="60" height="198" src="img/module-graph-simple.svg">
          </emu-figure>

          <p>Let's first assume that there are no error conditions. When a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> first calls <var>A</var>.LoadRequestedModules(), this will complete successfully by assumption, and recursively load the dependencies of <var>B</var> and <var>C</var> as well (respectively, <var>C</var> and none), and then set <var>A</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> = <var>B</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> = <var>C</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> = <emu-const>unlinked</emu-const>. Then, when the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> calls <var>A</var>.Link(), it will complete successfully (again by assumption) such that <var>A</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> = <var>B</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> = <var>C</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> = <emu-const>linked</emu-const>. These preparatory steps can be performed at any time. Later, when the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> is ready to incur any possible side effects of the modules, it can call <var>A</var>.Evaluate(), which will complete successfully, returning a Promise resolving to <emu-val>undefined</emu-val> (again by assumption), recursively having evaluated first <var>C</var> and then <var>B</var>. Each module's <var class="field">[[Status]]</var> at this point will be <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const>.</p>

          <p>Consider then cases involving linking errors, after a successful call to <var>A</var>.LoadRequestedModules(). If <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLinking"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLinking">InnerModuleLinking</a></emu-xref> of <var>C</var> succeeds but, thereafter, fails for <var>B</var>, for example because it imports something that <var>C</var> does not provide, then the original <var>A</var>.Link() will fail, and both <var>A</var> and <var>B</var>'s <var class="field">[[Status]]</var> remain <emu-const>unlinked</emu-const>. <var>C</var>'s <var class="field">[[Status]]</var> has become <emu-const>linked</emu-const>, though.</p>

          <p>Finally, consider a case involving evaluation errors after a successful call to Link(). If <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref> of <var>C</var> succeeds but, thereafter, fails for <var>B</var>, for example because <var>B</var> contains code that throws an exception, then the original <var>A</var>.Evaluate() will fail, returning a rejected Promise. The resulting exception will be recorded in both <var>A</var> and <var>B</var>'s <var class="field">[[EvaluationError]]</var> fields, and their <var class="field">[[Status]]</var> will become <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const>. <var>C</var> will also become <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const> but, in contrast to <var>A</var> and <var>B</var>, will remain without an <var class="field">[[EvaluationError]]</var>, as it successfully completed evaluation. Storing the exception ensures that any time a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> tries to reuse <var>A</var> or <var>B</var> by calling their Evaluate() method, it will encounter the same exception. (<emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">Hosts</a></emu-xref> are not required to reuse <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Records</a></emu-xref>; similarly, <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> are not required to expose the exception objects thrown by these methods. However, the specification enables such uses.)</p>

          <p>Now consider a different type of error condition:</p>

          <emu-figure id="figure-module-graph-missing" caption="A module graph with an unresolvable module" data-num="3">
            <img alt="A module graph in which module A depends on a missing (unresolvable) module, represented by ???" width="60" height="121" src="img/module-graph-missing.svg">
          </emu-figure>

          <p>In this scenario, module <var>A</var> declares a dependency on some other module, but no <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref> exists for that module, i.e. <emu-xref aoid="HostLoadImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-HostLoadImportedModule">HostLoadImportedModule</a></emu-xref> calls <emu-xref aoid="FinishLoadingImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-FinishLoadingImportedModule">FinishLoadingImportedModule</a></emu-xref> with an exception when asked for it. This could occur for a variety of reasons, such as the corresponding resource not existing, or the resource existing but <emu-xref aoid="ParseModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-parsemodule">ParseModule</a></emu-xref> returning some errors when trying to parse the resulting source text. <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">Hosts</a></emu-xref> can choose to expose the cause of failure via the completion they pass to <emu-xref aoid="FinishLoadingImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-FinishLoadingImportedModule">FinishLoadingImportedModule</a></emu-xref>. In any case, this exception causes a loading failure, which results in <var>A</var>'s <var class="field">[[Status]]</var> remaining <emu-const>new</emu-const>.</p>

          <p>The difference here between loading, linking and evaluation errors is due to the following characteristic:</p>
          <ul>
            <li><emu-xref aoid="Evaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-evaluation">Evaluation</a></emu-xref> must be only performed once, as it can cause side effects; it is thus important to remember whether evaluation has already been performed, even if unsuccessfully. (In the error case, it makes sense to also remember the exception because otherwise subsequent Evaluate() calls would have to synthesize a new one.)</li>
            <li>Linking, on the other hand, is side-effect-free, and thus even if it fails, it can be retried at a later time with no issues.</li>
            <li>Loading closely interacts with the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref>, and it may be desirable for some of them to allow users to retry failed loads (for example, if the failure is caused by temporarily bad network conditions).</li>
          </ul>

          <p>Now, consider a module graph with a cycle:</p>

          <emu-figure id="figure-module-graph-cycle" caption="A cyclic module graph" data-num="4">
            <img alt="A module graph in which module A depends on module B and C, but module B also depends on module A" width="181" height="121" src="img/module-graph-cycle.svg">
          </emu-figure>

          <p>Here we assume that the entry point is module <var>A</var>, so that the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> proceeds by calling <var>A</var>.LoadRequestedModules(), which performs <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLoading"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLoading">InnerModuleLoading</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>. This in turn calls <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLoading"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLoading">InnerModuleLoading</a></emu-xref> on <var>B</var> and <var>C</var>. Because of the cycle, this again triggers <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLoading"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLoading">InnerModuleLoading</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>, but at this point it is a no-op since <var>A</var>'s dependencies loading has already been triggered during this LoadRequestedModules process. When all the modules in the graph have been successfully loaded, their <var class="field">[[Status]]</var> transitions from <emu-const>new</emu-const> to <emu-const>unlinked</emu-const> at the same time.</p>

          <p>Then the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> proceeds by calling <var>A</var>.Link(), which performs <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLinking"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLinking">InnerModuleLinking</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>. This in turn calls <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLinking"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLinking">InnerModuleLinking</a></emu-xref> on <var>B</var>. Because of the cycle, this again triggers <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLinking"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLinking">InnerModuleLinking</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>, but at this point it is a no-op since <var>A</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> is already <emu-const>linking</emu-const>. <var>B</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> itself remains <emu-const>linking</emu-const> when control gets back to <var>A</var> and <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLinking"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLinking">InnerModuleLinking</a></emu-xref> is triggered on <var>C</var>. After this returns with <var>C</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> being <emu-const>linked</emu-const>, both <var>A</var> and <var>B</var> transition from <emu-const>linking</emu-const> to <emu-const>linked</emu-const> together; this is by design, since they form a strongly connected component. It's possible to transition the status of modules in the same SCC at the same time because during this phase the module graph is traversed with a depth-first search.</p>

          <p>An analogous story occurs for the evaluation phase of a cyclic module graph, in the success case.</p>

          <p>Now consider a case where <var>A</var> has a linking error; for example, it tries to import a binding from <var>C</var> that does not exist. In that case, the above steps still occur, including the early return from the second call to <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLinking"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLinking">InnerModuleLinking</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>. However, once we unwind back to the original <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleLinking"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-InnerModuleLinking">InnerModuleLinking</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>, it fails during InitializeEnvironment, namely right after <var>C</var>.ResolveExport(). The thrown <emu-val>SyntaxError</emu-val> exception propagates up to <var>A</var>.Link, which resets all modules that are currently on its <var>stack</var> (these are always exactly the modules that are still <emu-const>linking</emu-const>). Hence both <var>A</var> and <var>B</var> become <emu-const>unlinked</emu-const>. Note that <var>C</var> is left as <emu-const>linked</emu-const>.</p>

          <p>Alternatively, consider a case where <var>A</var> has an evaluation error; for example, its source code throws an exception. In that case, the evaluation-time analogue of the above steps still occurs, including the early return from the second call to <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>. However, once we unwind back to the original <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>, it fails by assumption. The exception thrown propagates up to <var>A</var>.Evaluate(), which records the error in all modules that are currently on its <var>stack</var> (i.e., the modules that are still <emu-const>evaluating</emu-const>) as well as via <var class="field">[[AsyncParentModules]]</var>, which form a chain for modules which contain or depend on top-level <code>await</code> through the whole dependency graph through the <emu-xref aoid="AsyncModuleExecutionRejected"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-async-module-execution-rejected">AsyncModuleExecutionRejected</a></emu-xref> algorithm. Hence both <var>A</var> and <var>B</var> become <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const> and the exception is recorded in both <var>A</var> and <var>B</var>'s <var class="field">[[EvaluationError]]</var> fields, while <var>C</var> is left as <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const> with no <var class="field">[[EvaluationError]]</var>.</p>

          <p>Lastly, consider a module graph with a cycle, where all modules complete asynchronously:</p>
          <emu-figure id="figure-module-graph-cycle-async" caption="An asynchronous cyclic module graph" data-num="5">
            <img alt="A module graph in which module A depends on module B and C, module B depends on module D, module C depends on module D and E, and module D depends on module A" width="241" height="211" src="img/module-graph-cycle-async.svg">
          </emu-figure>
          <p>Loading and linking happen as before, and all modules end up with <var class="field">[[Status]]</var> set to <emu-const>linked</emu-const>.</p>

          <p>Calling <var>A</var>.Evaluate() calls <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>, <var>B</var>, and <var>D</var>, which all transition to <emu-const>evaluating</emu-const>. Then <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref> is called on <var>A</var> again, which is a no-op because it is already <emu-const>evaluating</emu-const>. At this point, <var>D</var>.<var class="field">[[PendingAsyncDependencies]]</var> is 0, so <emu-xref aoid="ExecuteAsyncModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-execute-async-module">ExecuteAsyncModule</a></emu-xref>(<var>D</var>) is called and we call <var>D</var>.ExecuteModule with a new PromiseCapability tracking the asynchronous execution of <var>D</var>. We unwind back to the <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref> on <var>B</var>, setting <var>B</var>.<var class="field">[[PendingAsyncDependencies]]</var> to 1 and <var>B</var>.<var class="field">[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]]</var> to 1. We unwind back to the original <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var>, setting <var>A</var>.<var class="field">[[PendingAsyncDependencies]]</var> to 1. In the next iteration of the loop over <var>A</var>'s dependencies, we call <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref> on <var>C</var> and thus on <var>D</var> (again a no-op) and <var>E</var>. As <var>E</var> has no dependencies and is not part of a cycle, we call <emu-xref aoid="ExecuteAsyncModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-execute-async-module">ExecuteAsyncModule</a></emu-xref>(<var>E</var>) in the same manner as <var>D</var> and <var>E</var> is immediately removed from the stack. We unwind once more to the <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref> on <var>C</var>, setting <var>C</var>.<var class="field">[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]]</var> to 3. Now we finish the loop over <var>A</var>'s dependencies, set <var>A</var>.<var class="field">[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]]</var> to 4, and remove the entire strongly connected component from the stack, transitioning all of the modules to <emu-const>evaluating-async</emu-const> at once. At this point, the fields of the modules are as given in <emu-xref href="#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-1"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-1">Table 43</a></emu-xref>.</p>

          <emu-table id="table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-1" caption="Module fields after the initial Evaluate() call" data-num="43">

          <p>Let us assume that <var>E</var> finishes executing first. The fields of the updated modules are as given in <emu-xref href="#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-2"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-2">Table 44</a></emu-xref>.</p>

          <p><var>D</var> is next to finish (as it was the only module that was still executing). When that happens, <emu-xref aoid="AsyncModuleExecutionFulfilled"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-async-module-execution-fulfilled">AsyncModuleExecutionFulfilled</a></emu-xref> is called again and <var>D</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> is set to <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const>. Its ancestors available for execution are <var>B</var> (whose <var class="field">[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]]</var> is 1) and <var>C</var> (whose <var class="field">[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]]</var> is 3), thus <var>B</var> will be handled first: <var>B</var>.<var class="field">[[PendingAsyncDependencies]]</var> is decremented to become 0, <emu-xref aoid="ExecuteAsyncModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-execute-async-module">ExecuteAsyncModule</a></emu-xref> is called on <var>B</var>, and it starts executing. <var>C</var>.<var class="field">[[PendingAsyncDependencies]]</var> is also decremented to become 0, and <var>C</var> starts executing (potentially in parallel to <var>B</var> if <var>B</var> contains an <code>await</code>). The fields of the updated modules are as given in <emu-xref href="#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-3"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-3">Table 45</a></emu-xref>.</p>

The fields of the updated modules are as given in <emu-xref href="#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-4"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-4">Table 46</a></emu-xref>.</p>

At this point, the Promise in <var>A</var>.<var class="field">[[TopLevelCapability]]</var> (which was returned from <var>A</var>.Evaluate()) is resolved, and this concludes the handling of this module graph.           <p>Alternatively, consider a failure case where <var>C</var> fails execution and returns an error before <var>B</var> has finished executing. When that happens, <emu-xref aoid="AsyncModuleExecutionRejected"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-async-module-execution-rejected">AsyncModuleExecutionRejected</a></emu-xref> is called, which sets <var>C</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> to <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const> and <var>C</var>.<var class="field">[[EvaluationError]]</var> to the error. It then propagates this error to all of the AsyncParentModules by performing <emu-xref aoid="AsyncModuleExecutionRejected"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-async-module-execution-rejected">AsyncModuleExecutionRejected</a></emu-xref> on each of them. The fields of the updated modules are as given in <emu-xref href="#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-7"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-7">Table 49</a></emu-xref>.</p>

              </tr>

              </tr>

          <p><var>A</var> will be rejected with the same error as <var>C</var> since <var>C</var> will call <emu-xref aoid="AsyncModuleExecutionRejected"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-async-module-execution-rejected">AsyncModuleExecutionRejected</a></emu-xref> on <var>A</var> with <var>C</var>'s error. <var>A</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> is set to <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const>. At this point the Promise in <var>A</var>.<var class="field">[[TopLevelCapability]]</var> (which was returned from <var>A</var>.Evaluate()) is rejected. The fields of the updated module are as given in <emu-xref href="#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-8"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#table-module-graph-cycle-async-fields-8">Table 50</a></emu-xref>.</p>

          <p>Then, <var>B</var> finishes executing without an error. Any future importer of <var>B</var> will resolve the rejection of <var>B</var>.<var class="field">[[CycleRoot]]</var>.<var class="field">[[EvaluationError]]</var> from the evaluation error from <var>C</var> that was set on the cycle root <var>A</var>.               <p>GetExportedNames does not filter out or throw an exception for names that have ambiguous star export bindings.</p>
            </div></emu-note>

b7:Tb34,<emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ExportDeclaration" name="ExportDeclaration" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-ExportDeclaration">ExportDeclaration</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>export</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-NamedExports">NamedExports</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>;</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
        <ul>
          <li>
            It is a Syntax Error if the <emu-xref aoid="ReferencedBindings"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-static-semantics-referencedbindings">ReferencedBindings</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-NamedExports">NamedExports</a></emu-nt> contains any <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-StringLiteral">StringLiteral</a></emu-nt>s.
          </li>
          <li>
            For each <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt> <var>n</var> in the <emu-xref aoid="ReferencedBindings"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-static-semantics-referencedbindings">ReferencedBindings</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-NamedExports">NamedExports</a></emu-nt>: It is a Syntax Error if the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <var>n</var> is a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-ReservedWord">ReservedWord</a></emu-nt> or the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <var>n</var> is one of <emu-val>"implements"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"interface"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"let"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"package"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"private"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"protected"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"public"</emu-val>, or <emu-val>"static"</emu-val>.
          </li>
        </ul>
        <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>The above rule means that each <emu-xref aoid="ReferencedBindings"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-static-semantics-referencedbindings">ReferencedBindings</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-NamedExports">NamedExports</a></emu-nt> is treated as an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-IdentifierReference">IdentifierReference</a></emu-nt>.</p>
        </div></emu-note>

b9:T366e,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> ExportedNames takes no arguments and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of Strings.</p>
        <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>ExportedNames are the externally visible names that a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt> explicitly maps to one of its local name bindings.</p>
        </div></emu-note>

        <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p><emu-val>"\</emu-val>default\<emu-val>"</emu-val> is used within this specification as a synthetic name for anonymous default export values. See <emu-xref href="#note-star-default-star"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#note-star-default-star">this note</a></emu-xref> for more details.</p>

bb:T2639,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> ExportEntriesForModule takes argument <var>module</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>null</emu-val>) and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record">ExportEntry Records</a></emu-xref>. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
        <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ExportFromClause" name="ExportFromClause" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-ExportFromClause">ExportFromClause</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>*</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>entry</var> be the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#exportentry-record">ExportEntry Record</a></emu-xref> { <var class="field">[[ModuleRequest]]</var>: <var>module</var>, <var class="field">[[ImportName]]</var>: <emu-const>all-but-default</emu-const>, <var class="field">[[LocalName]]</var>: <emu-val>null</emu-val>, <var class="field">[[ExportName]]</var>: <emu-val>null</emu-val> }.</li><li class="exit">Return « <var>entry</var> ».</li></ol></emu-alg>

be:T1934,<p>The abstract operation InnerModuleLoading takes arguments <var>state</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#graphloadingstate-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#graphloadingstate-record">GraphLoadingState Record</a></emu-xref>) and <var>module</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>) and returns <emu-const>unused</emu-const>. It is used by LoadRequestedModules to recursively perform the actual loading process for <var>module</var>'s dependency graph. It performs the following steps when called:</p>

              <emu-alg><ol><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>state</var>.<var class="field">[[IsLoading]]</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li>If <var>module</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref>, <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[Status]]</var> is <emu-const>new</emu-const>, and <var>state</var>.<var class="field">[[Visited]]</var> does not contain <var>module</var>, then<ol><li>Append <var>module</var> to <var>state</var>.<var class="field">[[Visited]]</var>.</li><li>Let <var>requestedModulesCount</var> be the number of elements in <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[RequestedModules]]</var>.</li><li>Set <var>state</var>.<var class="field">[[PendingModulesCount]]</var> to <var>state</var>.<var class="field">[[PendingModulesCount]]</var> + <var>requestedModulesCount</var>.</li><li>For each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#modulerequest-record">ModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref> <var>request</var> of <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[RequestedModules]]</var>, do<ol><li>If <emu-xref aoid="AllImportAttributesSupported"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-AllImportAttributesSupported">AllImportAttributesSupported</a></emu-xref>(<var>request</var>.<var class="field">[[Attributes]]</var>) is <emu-val>false</emu-val>, then<ol><li>Let <var>error</var> be <emu-xref aoid="ThrowCompletion"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-throwcompletion">ThrowCompletion</a></emu-xref>(a newly created <emu-val>SyntaxError</emu-val> object).</li><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="ContinueModuleLoading"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-ContinueModuleLoading">ContinueModuleLoading</a></emu-xref>(<var>state</var>, <var>error</var>).</li></ol></li><li>Else if <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[LoadedModules]]</var> contains a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#loadedmodulerequest-record">LoadedModuleRequest Record</a></emu-xref> <var>record</var> such that <emu-xref aoid="ModuleRequestsEqual"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-ModuleRequestsEqual">ModuleRequestsEqual</a></emu-xref>(<var>record</var>, <var>request</var>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, then<ol><li>Perform InnerModuleLoading(<var>state</var>, <var>record</var>.<var class="field">[[Module]]</var>).</li></ol></li><li>Else,<ol><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="HostLoadImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-HostLoadImportedModule">HostLoadImportedModule</a></emu-xref>(<var>module</var>, <var>request</var>, <var>state</var>.<var class="field">[[HostDefined]]</var>, <var>state</var>).</li><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#note-step"><a c8:T3116,<p>The ResolveExport concrete method of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sourctextmodule-record">Source Text Module Record</a></emu-xref> <var>module</var> takes argument <var>exportName</var> (a String) and optional argument <var>resolveSet</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Records</a></emu-xref> with fields <var class="field">[[Module]]</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>) and <var class="field">[[ExportName]]</var> (a String)) and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record">ResolvedBinding Record</a></emu-xref>, <emu-val>null</emu-val>, or <emu-const>ambiguous</emu-const>.</p><p>ResolveExport attempts to resolve an imported binding to the actual defining module and local binding name. The defining module may be the module represented by the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref> this method was invoked on or some other module that is imported by that module. The parameter <var>resolveSet</var> is used to detect unresolved circular import/export paths. If a pair consisting of specific <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref> and <var>exportName</var> is reached that is already in <var>resolveSet</var>, an import circularity has been encountered. Before recursively calling ResolveExport, a pair consisting of <var>module</var> and <var>exportName</var> is added to <var>resolveSet</var>.</p>
                <p>If a defining module is found, a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record">ResolvedBinding Record</a></emu-xref> { <var class="field">[[Module]]</var>, <var class="field">[[BindingName]]</var> } is returned. This record identifies the resolved binding of the originally requested export, unless this is the export of a namespace with no local binding. In this case, <var class="field">[[BindingName]]</var> will be set to <emu-const>namespace</emu-const>. If no definition was found or the request is found to be circular, <emu-val>null</emu-val> is returned. If the request is found to be ambiguous, <emu-const>ambiguous</emu-const> is returned.</p>

            <p>It performs the following steps when called:</p>

Record</a></emu-xref>, set <var>done</var> to <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li>Set <var>requiredModule</var>.<var class="field">[[CycleRoot]]</var> to <var>module</var>.</li></ol></li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <var>index</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
              <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
                <p>A module is <emu-const>evaluating</emu-const> while it is being traversed by InnerModuleEvaluation. A module is <emu-const>evaluated</emu-const> on execution completion or <emu-const>evaluating-async</emu-const> during execution if its <var class="field">[[HasTLA]]</var> field is <emu-val>true</emu-val> or if it has asynchronous dependencies.</p>
              </div></emu-note>
              <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
                <p>Any modules depending on a module of an asynchronous cycle when that cycle is not <emu-const>evaluating</emu-const> will instead depend on the execution of the root of the cycle via <var class="field">[[CycleRoot]]</var>. This ensures that the cycle state can be treated as a single strongly connected component through its root module state.</p>
              </div></emu-note>

In <emu-xref aoid="InnerModuleEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-innermoduleevaluation">InnerModuleEvaluation</a></emu-xref>, the value of a module's <var class="field">[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]]</var> internal slot is unused when its <var class="field">[[EvaluationError]]</var> internal slot is not <emu-const>empty</emu-const>.</li><li>If <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[TopLevelCapability]]</var> is not <emu-const>empty</emu-const>, then<ol><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[CycleRoot]]</var> and <var>module</var> are the same <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>Perform ! <emu-xref aoid="Call"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-call">Call</a></emu-xref>(<var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[TopLevelCapability]]</var>.<var class="field">[[Reject]]</var>, <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>, « <var>error</var> »).</li></ol></li><li>For each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#cyclic-module-record">Cyclic Module Record</a></emu-xref> <var>ancestorModule</var> of <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[AsyncParentModules]]</var>, do<ol><li>Perform AsyncModuleExecutionRejected(<var>ancestorModule</var>, <var>error</var>).</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <emu-const>unused</emu-const>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
